Conclusion: 评估创意AI时需要区分"模型必须正确"和"模型应保持可操控性"两种场景，不能将收敛和分歧信号合并为单一指标。

Abstract: Modern AI evaluation frameworks treat evaluator disagreement as noise to be resolved. In creative domains, professional disagreement reflects genuine differences in taste, not measurement error. We argue that evaluating creative AI requires preserving two distinct signals: convergence, where professionals align around shared best practices, and divergence, where individual taste legitimately varies. We present the Human Creativity Benchmark (HCB), a benchmark that operationalizes this separation by collecting pairwise preferences, scalar ratings on prompt adherence, usability, and visual appeal, and qualitative rationale from domain professionals. Across 15,000 professional judgments spanning five creative domains and three workflow phases (ideation, mockup, refinement), we find that convergence concentrates on verifiable dimensions like technical correctness and visual hierarchy, while divergence concentrates on taste-driven dimensions like aesthetic direction and conceptual risk. No model excels uniformly across all phases. Collapsing these signals into a single quality metric discards the most actionable information: where models must be correct versus where they should remain steerable.

</details>


### [324] [DOPD: Dual On-policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30626)
*Xinlei Yu,Gen Li,Qingyi Si,Guibin Zhang,Yuqi Xu,Congcong Wang,Shuai Dong,Kaiwen Tuo,Xiangyu Zeng,Kaituo Feng,Qunzhong Wang,Yang Shi,Xiaobin Hu,Xiangyu Yue,Jiaqi Wang,Shuicheng Yan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出DOPD，一种优势感知的双重蒸馏范式，通过动态路由token级监督信号在特权教师和特权学生策略之间，以缓解特权幻觉问题，并在LLM和VLM任务上取得优于传统在线策略蒸馏的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线策略蒸馏（OPD）虽能通过密集的token级信号监督学生采样轨迹实现优越的能力迁移，但引入特权信息（无论是给教师还是学生）会导致"特权幻觉"——模型混淆了学生本应弥合的可迁移能力差距与只能模仿而无法真正复现的信息不对称差距。此外，token级监督固有的非均匀性（仅少量token携带关键能力信号）进一步放大了这一问题。

Method: 提出DOPD（Advantage-Aware Dual Distillation Paradigm），核心机制是基于优势差距和相对概率，在特权教师和特权学生策略之间动态路由token级监督。每个token根据其特点从教师或学生自身接收不同强度、目标和策略的监督信号，在传递可信能力的同时接收辅助信号，以缓解特权幻觉。

Result: 在LLM和VLM设置上的大量实验表明，DOPD持续优于传统OPD及其他对比方法。进一步在稳定性、鲁棒性、持续学习和分布外任务上的结果验证了其优越性。

Conclusion: DOPD通过优势感知的双重蒸馏范式有效解决了特权幻觉问题，为在线策略蒸馏提供了一种更优的性能边界提升方案。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) offers superior capacity transfer by supervising student-sampled trajectories with dense token-level signals. To furnish high-quality supervision sources and thereby elevate the performance frontier of distillation, an intuitive direction is to infuse privileged information to either teacher or student itself. However, this additional input induces a potential failure mode we dub privilege illusion: a pattern that conflates the transferable capability gap that students are meant to close, and the information asymmetry gap that can only be mimicked but never replicated. This issue is further amplified by the inherent non-uniformity of token-level supervision, where only a small subset of tokens carries pivotal capability-bearing signals. To this end, we propose DOPD, an advantage-aware dual distillation paradigm that dynamically routes token-level supervision between privileged teacher and privileged student policies based on their advantage gap and relative probabilities. Each token receives supervision of different strength, objective, and strategy from either teacher or student itself, which transfers credible capability while simultaneously receiving auxiliary signals, to alleviate privilege illusion. Extensive experiments on both large language model (LLM) and vision-language model (VLM) settings demonstrate that DOPD consistently outperforms Vanilla OPD and other counterparts. Further results on stability, robustness, continual learning, and out-of-distribution tasks validate its superiority.

</details>


### [325] [Self-Evolving World Models for LLM Agent Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30639)
*Xuan Zhang,Wenxuan Zhang,See-Kiong Ng,Yang Deng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: WorldEvolver是一种自进化世界模型框架，通过在部署时通过情景记忆、语义记忆和选择性预见三个模块修正上下文，在不改变下游智能体和模型参数的情况下，提升了世界模型的预测准确率和下游智能体的任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程LLM智能体需要世界模型提供前瞻性预测，但不可靠的预测可能被忽略甚至恶化决策。现有世界模型在部署时缺乏自我修正能力。

Method: 提出WorldEvolver框架，包含三个模块：(i) 情景记忆——通过检索式模拟利用真实动作转换；(ii) 语义记忆——从预测-观测不匹配中提取持久启发式规则；(iii) 选择性预见——在将预测整合到推理上下文前过滤低置信度预测。保持下游智能体和模型参数冻结。

Result: 在ALFWorld和ScienceWorld上评估，在Word2World上预测准确率最高，在AgentBoard上下游智能体成功率优于其他世界模型基线。

Conclusion: 测试时的记忆修正能同时提升世界模型的预测保真度和下游智能体的规划性能，验证了自进化框架的有效性。

Abstract: World models offer a principled way to equip long-horizon LLM agents with foresight: predictions of action consequences before execution. However, unreliable foresight can be ignored, misused, or even degrade downstream decision-making. In this paper, we introduce WorldEvolver, a self-evolving world model framework that revises its deployment-time context while keeping the downstream agent and all model parameters frozen. WorldEvolver integrates three modules: (i) Episodic Memory, which exploits real action transitions through retrieval-based simulation; (ii) Semantic Memory, which extracts persistent heuristic rules from prediction-observation mismatches; and (iii) Selective Foresight, which filters low-confidence predictions before integrating them into agent reasoning context. We evaluate WorldEvolver on ALFWorld and ScienceWorld, measuring world model prediction accuracy on Word2World and downstream agent success rate on AgentBoard. Extensive experiments show that WorldEvolver achieves the highest prediction accuracy across three backbones and leads other world model baselines on downstream agent success rate, demonstrating that test-time memory revision enhances both predictive fidelity and planning performance.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [326] [Comparing Scalar Objective Functions for Multi-Criteria Engineering Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28541)
*Olaf Frommann*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文比较了四种归一化双目标最小化标量化方法（加权和、成就标量函数、合意度函数和模糊逻辑公式），重点关注可达Pareto区域、参数诱导选择密度、补偿性、敏感性和可解释性。结果显示加权和简单但在凹Pareto前沿结构受限，而其他三种方法通过不同机制可达到非支撑内部区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多准则优化问题需要单一优选设计时，标量化函数的选择会影响折中方案的选取、偏好参数的解读以及非支撑Pareto区域的可达性，因此有必要系统比较不同标量化方法的特性。

Method: 采用两个解析定义的Pareto前沿（一个凸、一个凹），隔离目标函数公式与数值优化器行为的影响，从可达Pareto区域、参数诱导选择密度、准则间补偿性、敏感性和可解释性五个维度对比四种标量化方法。

Result: 加权和在凹前沿上结构受限；成就标量函数、合意度函数和模糊公式通过不同机制可到达内部非支撑区域；合意度函数引入非线性单准则偏好映射，模糊规则可表达非可分离和参考依赖的工程偏好。

Conclusion: 不同标量化方法在可达区域、补偿性和可解释性方面存在显著差异，选择需根据问题特性权衡，模糊和合意度函数在处理复杂偏好上更具灵活性。

Abstract: Scalar objective functions are required when a multi-criteria optimization problem must yield a single preferred design rather than only a Pareto set. The choice of scalarization influences which compromise is selected, how preference parameters are interpreted, and whether non-supported Pareto regions can be reached. This paper compares four formulations for normalized bi-criteria minimization: weighted sums, achievement scalarizing functions, desirability functions, and a fuzzy-logic-based formulation. Two analytically defined Pareto fronts, one convex and one concave, isolate the effect of the objective formulation from numerical optimizer behavior. The comparison focuses on reachable Pareto regions, parameter-induced selection density, compensation between criteria, sensitivity, and interpretability. Results show that weighted sums are simple but structurally limited on concave fronts, while achievement, desirability, and fuzzy formulations reach interior non-supported regions through different mechanisms. Desirability functions introduce nonlinear single-criterion preference mappings, whereas fuzzy rules express nonseparable and reference-dependent engineering preferences.

</details>


### [327] [A New Noise Model for Data-driven Control: Generalized Frobenius Norm Bounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28557)
*Huayuan Huang,M. Kanat Camlibel,Henk J. van Waarde*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的数据驱动控制噪声模型，该模型可视为Frobenius范数界的推广。针对瞬时有界噪声，该模型比基于二次矩阵不等式(QMI)的现有模型具有更小的保守性。利用新模型，推导出了数据驱动控制的充要条件，涵盖二次镇定、H2控制和H∞控制等广泛设计问题，并扩展到可镇定性和耗散性等分析问题。关键技术贡献是一种新的S-lemma，为向量化变量的二次不等式蕴含二次矩阵不等式提供充要条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据驱动控制中的噪声模型在处理瞬时有界噪声时过于保守，需要一种更精确的噪声描述方法来降低保守性，从而获得更高效的数据驱动控制条件。

Method: 提出一种新的噪声模型（Frobenius范数界的推广）；推导基于该模型的充要控制条件；提出新的S-lemma作为关键技术工具。

Result: 新模型在瞬时有界噪声下比QMI模型保守性更低；得到了数据驱动控制的充要条件（涵盖镇定、H2、H∞控制）；新S-lemma为向量化变量的二次不等式蕴含QMI提供了充要条件。

Conclusion: 新噪声模型及其配套的S-lemma为数据驱动控制提供了更精确且保守性更低的框架，可统一处理多种控制设计与分析问题。

Abstract: In this article, we introduce a new noise model for data-driven control. The model can be interpreted as a generalization of a Frobenius norm bound on the matrix of noise samples. For instantaneously bounded noise, the proposed model provides a less conservative overapproximation than an existing noise model based on a quadratic matrix inequality (QMI). Using the new model, we derive necessary and sufficient conditions for data-driven control. The framework covers a broad class of design problems, including quadratic stabilization, $\mathcal{H}_2$ control and $\mathcal{H}_{\infty}$ control, and is further extended to cover data-driven analysis problems, ranging for stabilizability to dissipativity. A key technical contribution is a new type of S-lemma that offers necessary and sufficient conditions under which a quadratic matrix inequality is implied by a quadratic inequality in vectorized variables.

</details>


### [328] [A Relaxation and Rectification (ReCR) Framework for Systems with Linear and Complementary Constraints: Theoretical Foundation, Algorithms and Numerical Experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28563)
*Wissam AlAli,Xin Jiang,Jiming Peng*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一套通用松弛理论（URT）将线性互补约束系统（SLCC）的可行性问题转化为等价的 lifted 空间中的双线性优化问题，并基于此开发了松弛-校正（ReCR）框架。该框架包含多种方法，能够在少量迭代内找到可行解或判定不可行性，数值实验验证了其在 LCP 等实例上的可靠性与扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SLCC 广泛存在于工程、经济及优化模型的最优性条件中，但一般情形下求解 SLCC 的可行解是 NP-hard 的，缺乏通用且有效的求解方法。

Method: 提出通用松弛理论（URT）将 SLCC 可行性等价转化为 lifted 空间中的双线性优化问题；推导其全局最优解的充要条件；基于此设计松弛-校正（ReCR）框架，包含多种子问题构造与工作空间选择不同的具体方法；并开发数值获取不可行性证书的流程。

Result: 在文献中的小规模和大规模 LCP 实例上，ReCR 方法通常在少量迭代内找到可行解；在更具挑战性的中规模 SLCC 实例上，混合 ReCR (H-ReCR) 表现出色。

Conclusion: 本文提出的 ReCR 框架为求解 SLCC 可行性问题提供了通用、可靠且可扩展的方法体系，在多个基准测试中优于现有求解器。

Abstract: Systems defined by linear and complementarity constraints (SLCCs) arise frequently in engineering, economics, and other related fields. They also appear in the optimality conditions of many challenging optimization models, such as bilinear optimization and linearly constrained quadratic optimization. It is known that finding a feasible solution to an SLCC is NP-hard in general. In this paper, we study the feasibility problem for a given SLCC: either find a feasible solution or determine that the system is infeasible. To this end, we introduce a universal relaxation theory (URT), which reformulates SLCC feasibility as an equivalent bilinear optimization problem with linear constraints in a lifted space. We then analyze the resulting bilinear model and derive necessary and sufficient optimality conditions for its global solutions. Based on these theoretical insights, we introduce a relaxation-rectification (ReCR) framework for finding a feasible solution to a given SLCC instance or certifying infeasibility. We develop several ReCR methods that differ in their working spaces and subproblem formulations and analyze their convergence properties. We also develop a numerical procedure for obtaining an infeasibility certificate when the ReCR methods do not find a feasible solution.
  We conduct numerical experiments to evaluate the reliability, robustness, and scalability of the proposed ReCR methods and compare them with existing SLCC solvers. On the tested small- and large-scale LCP instances from the literature, the proposed ReCR methods typically find feasible solutions in a few iterations. We also extend the benchmark with more challenging medium-scale SLCC instances, on which the proposed hybrid ReCR (H-ReCR) method exhibits promising performance.

</details>


### [329] [Robust Sparse Identification of Nonlinear Dynamics via Least Trimmed Squares](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28584)
*Fabio Amaral,Geovani N. Grapiglia,Cassio M. Oishi*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合ILTS和STLS的鲁棒SINDy框架（ILTS-SINDy），通过先剔除异常值再稀疏回归，在高达20%数据污染下仍能有效识别非线性动力学系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准SINDy方法对噪声和异常值敏感，现有鲁棒变体在异常值存在时性能有限，需要一种能有效解耦异常值过滤与稀疏回归的鲁棒辨识框架。

Method: 将ILTS（迭代最小修剪平方和）与STLS（序列阈值最小二乘）级联：ILTS通过迭代最小化最小平方残差和筛选可靠观测值，无需异常值先验知识；随后STLS恢复稀疏控制模型。

Result: 大量数值实验表明，在不同异常值污染水平下，ILTS-SINDy显著优于现有鲁棒SINDy变体，即使在20%观测值被污染的场景下仍能保持良好性能。

Conclusion: ILTS-SINDy通过解耦异常值过滤与稀疏回归，为含噪声和异常值的数据集提供了一种高效、鲁棒的非线性动力学辨识方案。

Abstract: In this work, we propose a robust Sparse Identification of Nonlinear Dynamics (SINDy) pipeline for handling datasets corrupted by noise and outliers. The method decouples outlier filtering from sparse regression by combining Iterative Least Trimmed Squares (ILTS) with Sequentially Thresholded Least Squares (STLS). Unlike standard approaches that treat all observations uniformly within a single regression stage, the proposed ILTS-SINDy framework first applies an ILTS procedure that iteratively minimizes the sum of the smallest squared residuals to identify the most reliable observations without prior knowledge of outliers, after which STLS is used to recover a parsimonious governing model. Extensive numerical experiments show that ILTS-SINDy can significantly outperform existing robust SINDy variants across a range of outlier contamination levels, with performance maintained even under settings with up to $20\%$ corrupted observations.

</details>


### [330] [Lipschitz Regularity in Wasserstein Robust Stochastic Optimal Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28633)
*Shengbo Wang,Jose Blanchet*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了Wasserstein鲁棒随机最优控制问题中值函数的Lipschitz正则性，证明在核鲁棒和噪声鲁棒两种模型下，Lipschitz假设条件能够保证鲁棒值函数具有Lipschitz连续性，揭示了Wasserstein鲁棒性不仅抗模型误配，还能正则化Bellman不动点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在鲁棒马尔可夫决策过程中，值函数的正则性（如Lipschitz连续性）对于近似和学习的可行性至关重要。非鲁棒场景下，即使奖励和转移动态满足Lipschitz条件，值函数也不一定具有Lipschitz性质，这促使作者探究Wasserstein鲁棒性是否能赋予值函数更好的正则性。

Method: 论文考虑两种鲁棒性形式化：核鲁棒模型（对手在Wasserstein球内扰动名义转移核的下一个状态分布）和噪声鲁棒模型（对手扰动状态转移动态的驱动噪声）。在无限时域折扣奖励准则下，对可能无界的Polish状态空间进行分析，从理论上证明Lipschitz模型原语可导出Lipschitz鲁棒值函数。

Result: 与直觉相反，Wasserstein鲁棒性使得原本在非鲁棒设定下不成立的Lipschitz值函数性质得以成立，即Lipschitz奖励和Lipschitz转移动态能保证鲁棒值函数的Lipschitz连续性。

Conclusion: Wasserstein鲁棒性不仅能抵御模型误配，还能对Bellman不动点起到正则化作用，从而在离散化、值函数逼近、估计和学习等任务中提供稳定性保障。

Abstract: Robust Markov decision processes provide a principled framework for protecting sequential decision-making against transition-law misspecification and have attracted substantial recent research interest. As in non-robust stochastic optimal control, an important question is whether the robust value function is sufficiently regular for approximation and learning. This paper studies Lipschitz regularity of optimal value functions for Wasserstein robust stochastic optimal control on possibly unbounded Polish state spaces under an infinite-horizon discounted reward criterion. We consider two robustness formulations: a kernel-robust model, in which the adversary perturbs the next-state distribution within a Wasserstein ball around a nominal transition kernel, and a noise-robust model, in which the adversary perturbs the driving noise of the state transition dynamics. In the non-robust setting, Lipschitz rewards and Lipschitz transition dynamics do not, in general, imply a Lipschitz value function. In contrast, we show that in these Wasserstein robust formulations, Lipschitz assumptions on the model primitives yield Lipschitz robust value functions. Thus, Wasserstein robustness not only protects against misspecification but also regularizes the Bellman fixed point, providing stability relevant to discretization, value-function approximation, estimation, and learning.

</details>


### [331] [Optimal control of diffusive mean-field models for swarming particles on the sphere](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28886)
*Jinwook Jung,Dohyun Kim*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了单位球面上带扩散的共识（高维Kuramoto型）动力学平均场最优控制问题，建立了微观N粒子Liouville问题的最优控制存在性，证明了固定控制下单粒子边际以O(1/√N)速率收敛到平均场解，并证明了微观最优控制弱极限是平均场问题的最优控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究共识动力学（高维Kuramoto模型）在扩散条件下的平均场最优控制，目标是通过漂移场和相互作用增益来跟踪给定目标密度，同时惩罚控制成本。

Method: 建立受控N粒子Liouville问题并证明最优控制存在性；对固定控制，采用定量随机平均场极限证明单粒子边际以O(1/√N)速率收敛；通过弱极限论证建立微观最优控制与平均场最优控制的关联。

Result: 固定控制下粒子级解以O(1/√N)速率收敛到平均场解；微观最优控制的任何弱极限都是平均场问题的最优控制。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了从微观N粒子系统到平均场极限的定量收敛率，并证明了微观最优控制向平均场最优控制的逼近性质，为高维共识动力学的控制问题提供了理论保证。

Abstract: We study a mean-field optimal control problem for a consensus (high-dimensional Kuramoto-type) dynamics with diffusion on the unit sphere. The control acts through a prescribed drift field and an interaction gain, and the cost functional is given to track a given target density while penalizing the control effort. At the microscopic level, we formulate the corresponding controlled $N$-particle Liouville problem and establish the existence of optimal controls. For fixed controls, we obtain a quantitative stochastic mean-field limit showing that the one-particle marginal converges to the mean-field solution with the convergence rate $\mathcal O(1/\sqrt{N})$. Finally, we show that microscopic optimal controls approximate a mean-field optimal control: any weak limit of particle-level minimizers is optimal for the mean-field problem.

</details>


### [332] [Distributed recursive binary identification under tampering and non-persistent excitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28913)
*Jian Guo,Ji-Feng Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于扩散策略的分布式递归投影算法，用于存在测量端篡改（二值观测标签可能被翻转）的分布式参数估计问题。在没有独立性、平稳性或高斯假设的条件下，建立了自适应预测器累积遗憾和分布式估计误差的几乎必然上界。在温和的协同激励条件下，即使每个节点单独不激励，所有节点的估计也是一致的。仿真验证了算法收敛性，而非协作和不知晓篡改的基线方法则不收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分布式参数估计方法大多假设观测为连续值且未被篡改，但实际场景中观测常为二值化且标签可能被恶意翻转。同时，现有理论通常依赖独立性、平稳性或高斯假设，且在节点单独不激励时估计不一致。本文旨在解决这些挑战。

Method: 基于扩散策略（diffusion strategy）开发了一种分布式递归投影算法（distributed recursive projection algorithm），节点间通过通信图交换信息进行协作估计。

Result: 建立了累积遗憾和分布式估计误差的几乎必然上界；在协同激励条件下证明了所有节点估计的一致性；仿真表明算法收敛，而基线方法不收敛。

Conclusion: 所提算法能有效应对测量端标签篡改的攻击，在弱假设下仍能保证分布式参数估计的一致性，且优于非协作和不知晓篡改的基线方法。

Abstract: In this paper, we consider distributed parameter estimation with binary observations under measurement-side tampering: each node observes a thresholded output whose label may be flipped and exchanges information over a communication graph. We develop a distributed recursive projection algorithm based on the diffusion strategy. Without imposing independence, stationarity, or Gaussian assumptions, we establish almost sure upper bounds of both the accumulated regrets of the adaptive predictor and the distributed estimation error. Under a mild cooperative excitation condition, all nodes' estimate are consistent, even when each node is individually non-exciting. Simulations on a jointly exciting network corroborate the theory and show that the proposed algorithm converges, whereas non-cooperative and tampering-unaware baselines do not.

</details>


### [333] [Second-Order Sensitivity of Efficient Solution and Marginal Maps in Parametric Vector Optimization with Set Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28965)
*N. X. D. Bao,Tan H. Cao*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对参数向量优化问题，建立了高效解映射的二阶灵敏度理论，在值-决策误差界条件下，将边际映射的二阶信息提升为解映射的二阶Dini公式，并针对结构化可行映射给出了显式公式，最终通过鲁棒多目标投资组合和电力市场DC调度模型进行了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有灵敏度理论多关注有效值（边际映射）的变化，缺乏从有效值到有效决策（解映射）的二阶信息传递机制。本文旨在建立高效解映射的二阶半可微性理论，填补这一理论空白。

Method: 首先在抽象包含模型 \(x \in H(p)\) 中，利用内外估计建立解映射 \(S\) 的二阶半可微性；然后针对结构化可行映射 \(H(p)=\{x \in \Omega : g(p,x) \in D\}\)，在Robinson度量正则性、二阶正则性和方向性二阶半可微性假设下，推导 \(\DD H\)、\(\DD \Phi\) 和 \(\DD S\) 的显式公式。

Result: 获得了高效解映射的二阶Dini公式；针对结构化约束系统给出了\(\DD H\)、\(\DD \Phi\)和\(\DD S\)的显式表达式；特别地，将理论应用于多面体不等式/等式系统，并通过鲁棒多目标投资组合模型和电力市场DC调度模型验证了框架的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文建立的高效解映射二阶灵敏度理论为参数向量优化问题的扰动分析提供了系统化的数学框架，可拓展至互补性约束等更广泛的问题类。

Abstract: We develop a second-order sensitivity theory for the efficient solution map \(S\) of a parametric vector optimization problem \(\min_C f(p,x)\) subject to \(x\in H(p)\). The main point is the passage from efficient values to efficient decisions. Under a value-to-decision error bound (VDB), second-order information for the marginal map \(Φ\) lifts to a second-order Dini formula for \(S\). We first work in the abstract inclusion model \(x\in H(p)\), where outer and inner estimates yield second-order semi-derivability of \(S\). We then specialize to structured feasible maps \(H(p)=\{x\inΩ:g(p,x)\in D\}\). Under Robinson metric regularity along \(Ω\), second-order regularity of \(Ω\) and \(D\), and directional second-order semi-derivability of the data, we obtain explicit formulas for \(\DD H\), \(\DDΦ\), and \(\DD S\). The framework is specialized to polyhedral inequality/equality systems and illustrated by a robust multi-objective portfolio model and a DC-dispatch model for electricity markets, with a brief discussion of complementarity-based extensions.

</details>


### [334] [Sharp $O(1/k)$ convergence rate for the Sinkhorn algorithm via a local analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28973)
*Guillaume Wang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 证明了Sinkhorn算法在$\ell_1$范数边际误差和联合相对熵上以$O(1/k)$速率收敛，该速率在渐近可缩放情形下是紧的。证明基于分析熵正则化最优传输问题的二分图，并区分最优传输计划中分配正质量的边与未分配正质量的边，从而得到具有紧速率的局部收敛界，再利用作者先前结果（arXiv:2604.26265）中几乎紧的速率（差对数因子）引导出全局界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立Sinkhorn算法在熵正则化最优传输问题中收敛速率的紧界，填补先前结果中存在的对数因子差距。

Method: 通过分析熵正则化最优传输问题的二分图结构，区分传输计划中正质量边与零质量边，导出局部紧速率收敛界，并结合作者之前几乎紧的全局结果进行自举提升。

Result: 证明了Sinkhorn算法在$\ell_1$范数边际误差和联合相对熵上达到$O(1/k)$的收敛速率，且该速率是紧的。

Conclusion: Sinkhorn算法的收敛速率确认为$O(1/k)$且不可改进，解决了该领域的一个紧界问题。

Abstract: We prove that the Sinkhorn algorithm converges at the rate of $O(1/k)$ in $\ell_1$-norm marginal error and in joint relative entropy, which is known to be sharp in the asymptotically scalable case. The proof is based on examining the bipartite graph associated to the entropy-regularized optimal transport problem, and treating differently the edges that are assigned a positive mass in the optimal transport plan vs. those that are not. This yields a local convergence bound with the sharp rate, which is bootstrapped into a global bound using the author's previous result in arXiv:2604.26265 where we showed an almost-sharp rate up to a logarithmic factor.

</details>


### [335] [Faster than Fast-LTS: Robust Regression and Outlier Detection with DC Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28974)
*Marah-Lisanne Thormann,Phan Tu Vuong,Alain B. Zemkoho,Tri-Dung Nguyen*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对最小修剪平方(LTS)稳健回归问题，将其重构为带 capped simplex 约束的凹最小化问题，提出逐次提升差分凸函数算法(sBDCA)，理论证明具有线性收敛速率，数值实验表明该方法比Fast-LTS快3.25倍且目标函数值降低90%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LTS稳健回归的精确解对应组合优化问题，计算成本极高；现有启发式Fast-LTS需要大量起始点才能获得稳健结果，存在改进空间。

Method: 将LTS问题重构为带capped simplex约束的凹最小化问题，提出逐次提升差分凸函数算法(sBDCA)；利用Łojasiewicz性质证明算法收敛性；推导并集成问题特定的预条件矩阵以确保单初始化的稳健性。

Result: sBDCA在最快情况下以线性速率收敛到局部解；相比Fast-LTS，速度提升达3.25倍，目标函数值降低高达90%，尤其在高维场景下表现突出。

Conclusion: sBDCA结合预条件处理是一种高效且稳健的LTS求解方法，代码开源，为Python稳健回归提供了实用指南。

Abstract: When datasets contain outliers, robust regression is a well-established alternative to Ordinary Least Squares. A commonly employed robust estimator is Least Trimmed Squares (LTS), which computes the regression coefficients from a subset of observations. Determining the exact solution corresponds to a combinatorial problem with prohibitive computational costs, even for instances of moderate dimension. Thus, the most prevalent approach in practice remains a heuristic known as Fast-LTS. Although the heuristic often performs effectively, certain elements of the approach remain open to improvement. In particular, its core procedure provides robust results only when initialized with a large number of starting points. To address the heuristic's limitations, this paper reformulates the LTS problem as a concave minimization problem subject to a capped simplex constraint, and proposes the successive Boosted Difference of Convex Functions Algorithm (sBDCA) as a solution method. Theoretically, we establish via the Łojasiewicz property that sBDCA converges to a local solution with a linear rate in the fastest case. To ensure robustness from a single initialization in practice, we derive and integrate a problem-specific preconditioning matrix into the algorithmic setup. Building on this theoretical foundation, we conduct numerical studies on various synthetic and real-world datasets to demonstrate the effectiveness of sBDCA with preconditioning. Specifically, we show that our approach is up to 3.25 times faster than Fast-LTS and achieves up to 90% lower objective function values, particularly in high-dimensional settings. As all code is openly available, this paper further provides a practical guide to robust regression in Python.

</details>


### [336] [An Approximate Conjugate Subgradient Algorithm with Matrix Parameter for Derivative-Free Nonsmooth Optimization Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29084)
*Morteza Kimiaei,Saman Babaie-Kafaki,Zohre Aminifard*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出了一种无导数矩阵共轭次梯度方法，用于求解局部Lipschitz函数的无约束非光滑优化问题，通过离散梯度构造Goldstein次微分模型并引入矩阵记忆校正与阻尼等机制，在数值实验中展示了良好的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对无约束非光滑优化问题，现有方法在缺乏梯度信息时鲁棒性不足，尤其在高维度和严格精度要求下表现有限，因此需要一种无需导数、能有效利用函数值信息且兼具鲁棒性和可靠性的优化方法。

Method: 利用函数值构造离散梯度，采样形成Goldstein次微分有限模型；计算凸包中最小范数元素作为平稳性度量与下降方向参考向量；引入矩阵记忆校正、系数阻尼、对角缩放、有界角校正和矩阵稳定性保障机制；采用带富集策略的两点线搜索以获取有效步长或改进局部模型。

Result: 在维度高达1000的非光滑测试问题上，两种变体在较低和中等精度要求下均表现鲁棒，其中矩阵共轭次梯度变体在最严格容差下最为可靠。

Conclusion: 该方法在适当的离散梯度近似与线搜索采样一致性假设下，能生成满足保障下降性质的方向并计算近似Goldstein平稳点，是一种有效的无导数非光滑优化方法。

Abstract: We propose a derivative-free matrix conjugate-subgradient method for unconstrained nonsmooth optimization of locally Lipschitz functions. The method constructs discrete gradients using only function values and forms a finite sampled model of the Goldstein subdifferential. A minimal-norm element of the convex hull of the sampled discrete gradients is then computed and used both as a stationarity measure and as the reference vector for generating descent-oriented directions. To improve robustness beyond the basic steepest-descent direction, we introduce a matrix memory correction together with coefficient damping, diagonal scaling, bounded-angle correction, and matrix-stability safeguards. A two-point line-search procedure with enrichment is used to obtain either a serious step or an improved local model. Under suitable consistency assumptions on the discrete-gradient approximation and line-search sampling, the method generates directions satisfying a safeguarded descent property and computes approximate Goldstein stationary points. Numerical experiments on nonsmooth test problems with dimensions up to 1000 show that both proposed variants are robust for lower and medium accuracy requirements, while the matrix conjugate-subgradient variant remains the most reliable under the strictest tolerance.

</details>


### [337] [Modern Theory of Gradient-Based Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29206)
*Bin Shi*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文综述了基于梯度优化的新兴技术，重点关注常微分方程视角与离散Lyapunov分析之间的相互作用。通过高分辨率ODE建模和Lyapunov函数系统构建，揭示了Nesterov加速梯度法和Polyak重型球法的加速机制（梯度校正项），并系统总结了NAG、FISTA等算法的收敛性、Lyapunov框架及其在ADMM、PDHG、极小极大优化等领域的统一扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深入理解梯度优化中的加速机制，特别是ODE视角与Lyapunov分析的理论联系，并为各类优化算法提供统一的分析框架。

Method: 采用高分辨率ODE建模和系统化Lyapunov函数构建，从连续ODE视角分析离散优化算法的加速机理，并将该方法推广到ADMM、PDHG及其加速变体。

Result: 1) 识别出梯度校正项是NAG-SC和重型球法加速的主要驱动力；2) 建立了NAG/FISTA的加速收敛（梯度范数、欠阻尼加速、强凸线性收敛）及广义加速方法的Lyapunov框架；3) 将ODE近似和Lyapunov技术统一扩展到ADMM、PDHG及极小极大优化。

Conclusion: ODE-Lyapunov分析框架为理解和设计加速优化算法提供了有力工具，并可扩展到更广泛的优化问题（如鞍点问题），未来方向聚焦于极小极大优化的Lyapunov分析。

Abstract: In this review, we offer a comprehensive survey of emerging techniques in gradient-based optimization, with a particular emphasis on the interplay between ordinary differential equation (ODE) perspectives and their extensions into discrete Lyapunov analysis. We begin by examining the acceleration mechanisms underlying Nesterov's accelerated gradient method for strongly convex functions (NAG-SC) and Polyak's heavy-ball method, identifying the gradient-correction term as the primary driver of acceleration. This mechanistic insight is substantiated through high-resolution ODE modeling and the systematic construction of Lyapunov functions. We then synthesize recent advancements in convex optimization regarding NAG and its proximal generalization, the fast iterative shrinkage-thresholding algorithm (FISTA). Key topics include the accelerated convergence of gradient norms, underdamped acceleration, linear convergence under strong convexity, and novel Lyapunov frameworks for establishing convergence and monotonicity properties of generalized accelerated methods. Furthermore, we demonstrate how these ODE approximations and Lyapunov techniques can be extended to provide a unified framework for analyzing advanced optimization algorithms, including the alternating direction method of multipliers (ADMM), the primal-dual hybrid gradient (PDHG) method, and their respective accelerated variants. Finally, we discuss recent progress in minimax optimization and outline future directions for extending Lyapunov-based analysis to saddle-point problems.

</details>


### [338] [Approximation and Controllability of Nonlinear Control-Affine Systems via Semiautonomous Neural Ordinary Differential Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29338)
*Dev Prakash Jha,Raju K. George*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出受控半自主神经ODE（controlled SA-NODE），用于近似和学习非线性受控动力学系统。该方法将半自主神经ODE扩展到控制仿射系统，通过时不变可训练系数降低参数复杂度。证明了通用逼近定理、量化逼近误差界（O(P^{-1/2}+Q^{-1/2})）以及近似可控性保持性质。在受控摆和Duffing振子实验上展示了优于经典神经ODE的参数量效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的神经ODE方法在处理受控（带外部控制输入）非线性系统时，参数复杂度较高，且缺乏理论保证。作者希望构建一种既能有效逼近受控动力学系统、又具有较低参数复杂度，同时具备可控性保持理论保证的方法。

Method: 提出controlled SA-NODE架构，将半自主神经ODE推广至控制仿射系统，使用时不变系数降低参数复杂度；理论上证明通用逼近定理、基于Sobolev和Barron正则性的量化误差估计（O(P^{-1/2}+Q^{-1/2})）以及近似可控性保持；数值实验在受控摆和Duffing振子系统上验证。

Result: 理论层面：建立了通用逼近定理、量化逼近误差界、可控性保持性质。实验层面：在受控摆和Duffing振子系统中，controlled SA-NODE以显著更少的可训练参数实现了准确的轨迹重构和可控性性能。

Conclusion: controlled SA-NODE是一种高效、有理论保证的受控非线性系统逼近与学习方法，在参数效率上优于经典神经ODE架构，同时保持了近似可控性。

Abstract: In this paper, we introduce controlled semiautonomous neural ordinary differential equations (controlled SA-NODEs) for the approximation and learning of nonlinear controlled dynamical systems. The proposed framework extends semiautonomous neural ODEs to control-affine systems while preserving reduced parameter complexity through time-independent trainable coefficients. We establish a universal approximation theorem showing that controlled SA-NODEs approximate trajectories of nonlinear controlled systems uniformly on compact sets of initial conditions and admissible controls. Under additional Sobolev and Barron regularity assumptions, we derive quantitative approximation estimates of order $\mathcal{O}(P^{-1/2}+Q^{-1/2})$. We further prove that approximate controllability properties of the original nonlinear system are preserved under the controlled SA-NODE approximation. Numerical experiments on controlled pendulum and Duffing oscillator systems demonstrate that the proposed framework achieves accurate trajectory reconstruction and controllability performance with significantly fewer trainable parameters than classical neural ODE architectures.

</details>


### [339] [Turnpike and Sparse Optimal Control for Semiautonomous Neural ODEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29343)
*Dev Prakash Jha,Raju K. George*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了带ℓ¹正则化控制的控制仿射半自主神经常微分方程(SA-NODE)的长期最优控制问题，证明了指数收费公路性质、单边时间稀疏性和积分收费公路估计，数值实验展示30倍参数缩减且不损失稳定性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究SA-NODE长期最优控制问题，探索ℓ¹正则化诱导的稀疏性结构及其与收费公路性质的关联，旨在实现参数高效的控制策略。

Method: 结合耗散性不等式、庞特里亚金最优性系统的一致伴随界以及针对半自主架构的时间尺度重标论证。

Result: 最优状态-控制对满足指数收费公路性质；ℓ¹惩罚诱导单边时间稀疏性（控制在前段[0,T*]满幅活跃，之后恒为零）；积分收费公路估计表明时间平均偏差一致有界；Duffing振子和阻尼摆数值实验验证了三阶段收费公路轮廓和单边稀疏结构，实现30倍参数缩减。

Conclusion: SA-NODE结合ℓ¹正则化能够在长期最优控制中同时实现指数收费公路性质和单边时间稀疏性，相比标准NODE实现显著参数缩减而不损失稳定性能。

Abstract: We study long-time optimal control of control-affine semiautonomous neural ordinary differential equations (SA-NODEs) with $\ell^1$-regularized controls. Three results are established. First, optimal state-control pairs satisfy an \emph{exponential turnpike property}: they remain exponentially close to a stationary optimal pair for most of the time horizon, with decay rate and prefactor independent of the horizon length $T$. Second, $\ell^1$ penalisation induces \emph{one-sided temporal sparsity}: optimal controls are active at full amplitude on an initial arc $[0,T^*]$ and vanish identically on $(T^*,T)$, where $T^*$ is independent of $T$ for $T$ large. Third, an integral turnpike estimate shows the time-averaged deviation from the stationary pair is bounded uniformly in $T$. The proofs combine dissipativity inequalities, uniform adjoint bounds via the Pontryagin optimality system, and a time-rescaling argument adapted to the semiautonomous architecture. Numerical experiments on a Duffing oscillator and a damped pendulum confirm the three-phase turnpike profile and the one-sided sparsity structure, and demonstrate a $30\times$ parameter reduction over vanilla NODEs with no loss of stabilization performance.

</details>


### [340] [An Exact Algorithm for Mixed-Integer Bilevel Stochastic Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29344)
*Tomás Lagos,Dmytro Matsypura*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 针对混合整数双层随机规划问题，提出一种结合确定性值函数重构与随机场景分解的精确算法，通过随机次梯度割平面方案动态生成追随者最优性割并更新拉格朗日乘子，在有限时间内收敛到全局最优解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合整数双层随机规划问题由于层级结构和双层离散变量，本质上是Σ₂ᵖ-难的，标准单层重构方法计算上不可行，亟需高效的精确求解算法。

Method: 提出扩展单层重构及其松弛形式实现场景分解；设计随机次梯度割平面方案，动态生成追随者最优性割并更新拉格朗日乘子；结合确定性值函数重构与随机场景分解。

Result: 在有界性假设下，算法在有限时间内收敛到真全局最优解，且在整个执行过程中提供有效的上下界。

Conclusion: 该算法为求解混合整数双层随机规划这一困难问题提供了首个在有限时间内收敛到全局最优的精确方法。

Abstract: We study a class of mixed-integer bilevel stochastic programs where the leader commits to a first-stage decision before uncertainty is realized, and the follower solves a subsequent mixed-integer optimization problem for each revealed scenario. Due to the hierarchical structure and the presence of discrete variables at both levels, these problems are inherently $Σ_2^p$-hard, making standard single-level reformulations computationally intractable. To address this significant computational challenge, we develop an exact algorithm that combines deterministic value-function reformulations with stochastic scenario-wise decomposition. Specifically, we propose an extended single-level reformulation and a corresponding relaxation that enable scenario decomposition. We then introduce a stochastic subgradient cutting-plane scheme that dynamically generates follower optimality cuts while updating the Lagrange multipliers. We prove that, under boundedness assumptions, our algorithm converges in finite time to a true global optimum while providing valid upper and lower bounds throughout its execution.

</details>


### [341] [Solver-Verified Formulation Generation and Selection for Multi-Warehouse Inventory Allocation Using Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29366)
*Jintao Xu,Yingzheng Ma,Jiong Dong,Yongzhi Qi,Jianshen Zhang,Dongyang Geng,Anni Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出ORLA框架，结合大语言模型与运筹学方法，自动从文本需求生成并优化多仓库库存分配方案，在京东29个生产批次上实现4.5个百分点的分配精度提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模电商供应链中多仓库库存分配的需求常以半结构化或自然语言形式表达，而非可直接求解的运筹学公式，现有方法难以灵活应对场景依赖的分配约束。

Method: 提出ORLA框架，包含三个核心组件：1) 自动"问题-模型-代码(PMC)"生成，利用LLM从文本生成候选OR公式和求解器代码；2) 基于学习的公式选择，预估候选公式质量并择优；3) 可行性恢复机制，针对不可行情况引入惩罚松弛。开发了三类MIP公式族：偏差最小化、软带约束、背包启发式分配。

Result: 在京东29个生产评估批次上，最优单一OR公式相比现有方法提升3.4个百分点，完整ORLA框架提升4.5个百分点，并在26/29批次中改善分配精度。

Conclusion: OR-guided LLM框架能有效弥合自然语言需求与运筹优化求解之间的鸿沟，通过求解器反馈闭环迭代提升分配质量，具有实际部署价值。

Abstract: Balance-oriented multi-warehouse inventory allocation is a recurring decision problem in large-scale e-commerce supply chains, in which a fixed replenishment quantity is distributed across warehouses to balance post-allocation inventory coverage while accounting for demand forecasts and heterogeneous allocation constraints. In practice, allocation requirements are often scenario-dependent and expressed in semi-structured or natural-language form rather than as ready-to-solve operations research (OR) formulations. We propose an OR-guided Large Language Model (LLM) for Allocation (ORLA) that uses solver feedback to generate, verify, and select OR formulations. ORLA integrates automatic "Problem-Model-Code (PMC)" generation, learning-based formulation selection, and feasibility restoration. We develop three complementary mixed-integer programming formulation families based on deviation minimization, soft band compliance, and knapsack-inspired allocation, together with solver-ready mixed-integer linear programming reformulations, modular constraint extensions, and a penalty-based relaxation mechanism for infeasible cases. The LLM component generates candidate formulations and executable solver code from textual or semi-structured specifications, while the solver provides verification signals for executability, feasibility, and solution quality. To address instance heterogeneity, ORLA estimates the expected quality of candidate formulations, selects promising candidates, and combines their outputs through score-aware aggregation. Experimental results on 29 production evaluation batches from JD.com show that the best single OR formulation improves allocation accuracy by 3.4 percentage points over the incumbent approach, while the full ORLA framework achieves a 4.5 percentage-point overall improvement and improves allocation accuracy in 26 of the 29 evaluation batches.

</details>


### [342] [Fractional Stochastic Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29438)
*Yuecai Han,Jianming Xu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种分数阶随机神经网络，其残差动力学由分数布朗运动驱动。通过引入离散随机最大值原理构建伴随递归，证明了投影样本随机梯度下降的均方收敛性。数值实验包括闭式收敛测试、带不确定性量化的噪声回归、长记忆时间序列生成和结构化扰动下的图像分类。结果表明，在某些设置下，分数阶驱动相比布朗运动和确定性基线能改善长记忆恢复和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在利用分数布朗运动（fBm）的特性来改进传统随机神经网络，特别是针对具有长记忆性或需要更强鲁棒性的任务，探索分数阶随机驱动在神经网络动态建模中的优势。

Method: 1) 构建由分数布朗运动驱动的残差动力学随机神经网络；2) 引入离散随机最大值原理，推导对应的伴随递归；3) 针对确定性网络参数，证明投影样本随机梯度下降的均方收敛性；4) 通过多种数值实验验证方法有效性。

Result: 数值实验在闭式收敛测试、带不确定性量化的噪声回归、长记忆时间序列生成和结构化扰动下的图像分类等任务中，验证了分数阶驱动在长记忆恢复和鲁棒性方面优于布朗运动和确定性基线。

Conclusion: 分数随机神经网络在长记忆恢复和鲁棒性方面具有优势，为处理复杂随机动力学的深度学习模型提供了新的理论框架和实践验证。

Abstract: In this paper, we develop a fractional stochastic neural network with residual dynamics driven by fractional Brownian motion. By introducing a discrete stochastic maximum principle for the network, we construct the corresponding adjoint recursion. For deterministic network parameters, we prove mean square convergence of projected samplewise stochastic gradient descent. Numerical experiments include a closed form convergence test, noisy regression with uncertainty quantification, long memory time series generation and image classification under structured perturbations. The results identify settings in which fractional drivers improve long memory recovery or robustness relative to Brownian and deterministic baselines.

</details>


### [343] [A Posteriori Error Analysis for Decoupled Neural Approximations of Fully Coupled FBSDEs with Control Mismatch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29474)
*Xichuan Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对全耦合正倒向随机微分方程（FBSDEs）的解耦神经近似，建立了一套后验误差分析框架。通过引入辅助控制过程处理解耦带来的控制不匹配问题，推导了可计算的后验误差界，并在线性和非线性数值实验中验证了所提指标的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习实践中常采用解耦神经近似来求解全耦合FBSDEs，但这种解耦会产生控制不匹配问题，现有误差分析框架未能涵盖这一因素。本文旨在建立包含控制不匹配项的后验误差分析理论，为可重复和一致性的数值近似提供理论保障。

Method: 首先建立全耦合FBSDEs在漂移、扩散、生成项、终端条件和辅助控制输入扰动下的连续时间稳定性估计；然后将该估计推广到离散时间情形，推导仅依赖终端缺陷、路径残差和控制不匹配的可计算后验误差界；辅助控制与反向逼近一致时，不匹配项消失，误差界退化为标准两项形式。

Result: 在线性-二次型FBSDE（有精确参考解）和多维Burgers型FBSDE（无精确参考解）上的数值实验表明，所提出的后验误差指标能够有效诊断数值近似的误差来源，控制不匹配惩罚项对确保解耦神经近似的一致性和可重复性具有重要贡献。

Conclusion: 本文为解耦神经近似全耦合FBSDEs提供了严格的后验误差分析框架，控制不匹配项是误差界的关键组成部分；该框架在数值实践中可作为诊断工具，提升深度学习求解FBSDEs的可靠性和可重复性。

Abstract: This paper develops an a posteriori error analysis framework for decoupled neural approximations of fully coupled forward--backward stochastic differential equations (FBSDEs). It provides an a posteriori error-analysis for the idealized discrete adapted trajectory. The main feature of the proposed formulation is the use of an auxiliary control process in the forward coefficients, which may differ from the backward component approximated by the neural network. This decoupling is useful in practical deep learning implementations, but it creates a control mismatch that must be included in the error analysis. We first establish a continuous-time stability estimate for fully coupled FBSDEs under perturbations of the drift, diffusion, generator, terminal condition, and auxiliary control input. We then transfer this estimate to the discrete-time setting and derive computable a posteriori error bounds depending only on the terminal defect, the pathwise residual, and the control mismatch. When the auxiliary control is identified with the backward approximation, the mismatch term vanishes and the bound reduces to the standard two-term form. Numerical experiments on a linear--quadratic FBSDE with an explicit reference solution and a multidimensional Burgers-type FBSDE without a reference solution illustrate the diagnostic role of the proposed indicators and the contribution of the mismatch penalty to the consistency and reproducibility of the numerical approximations.

</details>


### [344] [The Simple Strategy-Iteration Method is Strongly Polynomial for the Turn-Based Deterministic Forward Game](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29568)
*Sanyou Mei,Chunlin Sun,Yinyu Ye*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究转向确定性前向博弈（TBDFGs），证明即使不利用该结构信息，通用策略迭代算法仍具有强多项式时间保证；当已知前向结构时，提出的反向SCC传播算法可获得更好的复杂度上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索转向确定性零和博弈中"前向条件"（禁止混合玩家反馈环）这一结构性质对算法收敛性的影响，分离该性质在无结构感知和有结构感知两种情形下的算法复杂度。

Method: 1) 分析通用策略迭代方法在TBDFG上的运行，证明其无需知晓结构信息仍具有强多项式保证（O(n^6m^4log^4 n)次单纯形枢轴步骤）；2) 提出反向SCC传播算法，利用已知的TBDFG结构，通过求解一系列确定性MDP子问题达到更优的O(n^3m^2log^2 n)复杂度上界。

Result: 前向条件可作为结构证书，使无结构感知的策略迭代方法在该类博弈上获得强多项式收敛保证；已知结构时，反向SCC传播算法进一步将复杂度降低。

Conclusion: 前向结构既能使通用策略迭代方法规则化收敛，又能支持更高效的结构感知算法。

Abstract: We study Turn-Based Deterministic Forward Games (TBDFGs), the subclass of turn-based deterministic zero-sum games in which no directed cycle contains actions controlled by both players. This forward condition is strictly weaker than acyclicity: recurrent behavior may be arbitrarily rich within one player's states, while mixed-player feedback cycles are excluded. Our main contribution separates two algorithmic consequences of this structure. First, we analyze the simple strategy-iteration method of [11,14], a generic method for TBSGs whose execution neither tests for nor uses the TBDFG property. We prove that this structure-oblivious algorithm nevertheless has a strongly polynomial guarantee on every TBDFG. In particular, it terminates after at most $O(n^6m^4\log^4 n)$ simplex pivot steps. Thus, the forward property acts as a structural certificate for convergence even when the algorithm is not informed that the input has this property. Second, when the TBDFG structure is known in advance, a backward SCC propagation algorithm is proposed that solves a sequence of deterministic-MDP subproblems and improves the bound to $O(n^3m^2\log^2 n)$ simplex pivot steps. Together, these results show that forward structure both regularizes the convergence of a general strategy-iteration method and supports a sharper structure-aware algorithm.

</details>


### [345] [AdaGrad does not adapt to Hölder-smoothness for composite objectives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29893)
*Matia Bojovic,Saverio Salzo,Massimiliano Pontil*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文发现了一个简单的一维确定性凸复合优化问题，其中AdaGrad算法无法达到Hölder光滑目标函数经典收敛率$\mathcal{O}(n^{-(1+ν)/2})$，原因是光滑项的梯度在最优解处不为零导致步长过度衰减，并讨论了基于梯度映射或连续梯度差的累积机制如何避免这一问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示AdaGrad算法在凸复合优化中存在的根本性缺陷，即步长累积机制与复合最优性之间的不匹配，以及光滑项梯度在最优解处非零带来的负面影响。

Method: 构造一个简单的一维确定性凸复合优化反例，理论分析AdaGrad在该问题上的收敛行为，并与基于梯度映射或连续梯度差的替代累积机制进行对比。

Result: AdaGrad在该反例中因梯度在最优解处不为零而持续过度减小步长，导致收敛速度慢于Hölder光滑目标的理论下界；而梯度映射或连续梯度差等累积机制可以避免这一病理现象。

Conclusion: AdaGrad的经典步长累积方式不适用于复合优化场景，需要改进累积机制以适配复合结构。

Abstract: We exhibit a simple deterministic one-dimensional convex composite optimization problem for which AdaGrad scheme does not achieve the classical convergence rate $\mathcal{O}(n^{-(1+ν)/2})$ associated with Hölder-smooth objectives. The example highlights a basic mismatch between classical AdaGrad accumulation and composite optimality. A main insight is that the gradient of the smooth term may not vanish at the optimum, causing AdaGrad to keep reducing its stepsize excessively and converge more slowly. We also discuss why alternative accumulation mechanisms based on gradient mappings or on successive gradient differences, avoid this pathology.

</details>


### [346] [Semi-discrete convex order and Laguerre tessellation fitting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29913)
*David P. Bourne,Thomas Gallouët,Quentin Mérigot,Andrea Natale*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究从给定胞体重心重建具有指定胞体体积的Laguerre镶嵌问题，建立了该问题与Wasserstein投影的几何联系，并应用于材料科学中的EBSD图像拟合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Laguerre镶嵌是参数化凸分割的高效工具，在计算几何、成像和数值分析中广泛应用。然而，给定胞体体积和重心反推Laguerre镶嵌的问题尚未得到充分研究，该问题的解决有助于更灵活地利用Laguerre镶嵌进行建模和数据分析。

Method: 将重建问题转化为在凸序支配关系下寻找离散测度的Wasserstein投影问题，通过建立绝对连续测度与离散测度的凸序关系，近似求解Laguerre镶嵌的重建。该方法也可推广到任意重心集合的拟合。

Result: 建立了Laguerre镶嵌重建问题与Wasserstein投影之间的几何对应关系，证明了可通过计算Wasserstein投影近似求解。在材料科学中成功应用于钢铁EBSD图像的Laguerre镶嵌拟合。

Conclusion: 本文提出的方法为从胞体体积和重心重建Laguerre镶嵌提供了理论与算法框架，并展示了在材料显微结构分析中的实用价值。

Abstract: Laguerre tessellations offer an efficient way to parameterize a large class of convex partitions of Euclidean space using only a set of points and scalar weights. For this reason, they have become popular in computational geometry, imaging and numerical analysis, both as a modeling and a discretization tool. In this paper we study the problem of reconstructing a Laguerre tessellation with prescribed cell volumes from the barycenters of its cells. We establish a geometric interpretation of this problem in terms of the set of discrete measures dominated in convex order by an absolutely continuous measure. In particular, we show that the reconstruction problem can be solved approximately by computing a Wasserstein projection onto this set. More generally, our method can also be applied to fit a Laguerre tessellation to an arbitrary set of barycenters. We give a concrete application of this in materials science, of fitting a Laguerre tessellation to an electron backscatter diffraction (EBSD) image of a steel.

</details>


### [347] [Factorized low-rank matrix recovery problem, Schatten-$q$ quasi-norm, Error bound for critical point, Kurdyka-Łojasiewicz property, Inexact proximal alternating linearized minimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29973)
*Yongjun Chen,Defeng Sun,Liping Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对Schatten-q拟范数正则化的低秩矩阵恢复问题的因子化模型，研究了临界点的性质（引入S-临界点概念并建立误差界），提出了基于不精确近端交替线性化最小化方法（含可计算的\|·\|_2^q不精确近端算子），证明了全局收敛性和线性收敛率（KL指数1/2），并通过数值实验验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有Schatten-q拟范数正则化的因子化模型存在两个未解决问题：(i) 临界点的误差界尚未被探索；(ii) 对于一般q，\|·\|_2^q的近端算子缺乏闭式解，导致算法只能采用固定的q值（如1/2或2/3）。本文旨在同时解决这两个问题。

Method: (1) 理论分析：证明因子化模型中Schatten-q拟范数隐式赋予临界点列正交性，引入S-临界点概念，推导其与真实矩阵间的误差界；(2) 算法设计：提出不精确近端交替线性化最小化方法，给出\|·\|_2^q的实用可计算的不精确近端算子及满足不精确条件的判别准则；(3) 收敛性分析：在KL条件下建立全序列收敛性与收敛率，证明最小二乘损失下S-临界点处KL指数为1/2，进而推出线性收敛。

Result: (1) 全局最优点必为S-临界点，且S-临界点与真实矩阵存在误差界；(2) 提出了可处理一般q的不精确近端算子，突破了固定q的限制；(3) 建立了算法的全局收敛性及线性收敛率；(4) 数值实验验证了算法的有效性和理论性质。

Conclusion: 本文从理论和算法两方面完善了Schatten-q拟范数因子化模型：理论上揭示了列正交性这一关键性质并建立了误差界；算法上通过不精确近端策略解决了通用q值的计算难题，并给出了严格的收敛性保证。

Abstract: The Schatten-$q$ quasi-norm is a widely used nonconvex rank surrogate and matrix factorization is an effective approach to reduce computational cost. In this paper, we consider the equivalent group-sparse factorized reformulation of Schatten-$q$ norm regularized low-rank matrix recovery problem. Though this factorized model exhibits favorable performance, two issues remain: (i) the error bound of critical points is unexplored; (ii) the proximal operator of $\|\cdot\|_2^q$ lacks a closed-form solution for general $q$, limiting algorithms to adopt fixed $q$ like $1/2$ or $2/3$. This paper addresses both issues. We investigate the properties of critical points for the factorized problem and show that, compared to nuclear norm, the Schatten-$q$ norm implicitly endows critical points with column orthogonality. From this insight, we introduce the notion of S-critical points under mild conditions that ensure column orthogonality with easily operable criterion for identifying. We show that global optimal points must be S-critical points and we derive an error bound between S-critical points and the true matrix. We further present an inexact proximal alternating linearized minimization method for the factorized problem, along with practically computable inexact proximal operator for $\|\cdot\|_2^q$ and criteria to find solutions satisfying inexactness conditions, and we establish the whole sequence convergence and a convergence rate guarantee under Kurdyka--Łojasiewicz condition. Moreover, we prove that the factorized model with least-squares loss has KL exponent $1/2$ at S-critical points, then the iteration converges linearly under suitable condition. Extensive numerical experiments validate the effectiveness of our algorithm and confirm the theoretical properties of the factorized model.

</details>


### [348] [A Restart-Free Accelerated Algorithm for Non-Convex Minimization: Continuous and Discrete Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30050)
*Kansei Ushiyama,Shun Sato*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出两种无需重启机制、不依赖ε作为输入参数的一阶方法，以O(ε^{-7/4})的复杂度找到非凸函数的ε-近似一阶稳定点，并通过ODE离散化推导，在PEP框架下进行并行分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖重启机制以达到O(ε^{-7/4})复杂度，且需要ε作为输入参数或预先知道Lipschitz常数，限制了实用性和实现简便性。

Method: 通过离散化新的ODE连续时间模型，在PEP框架下并行分析连续和离散收敛性；算法1简单实现且末次迭代对初值可微；算法2自适应估计Lipschitz常数，无需先验知识。

Result: 两种算法在O(ε^{-7/4})次函数和梯度求值内达到ε-一阶稳定点；算法2在数值实验中优于现有无参数方法。

Conclusion: 无重启设计的算法在实现简便性、可微性和适应性方面具有优势，为非凸优化提供新的高效求解框架。

Abstract: We propose two novel first-order methods for minimizing nonconvex functions with Lipschitz-continuous gradients and Hessians. These algorithms attain an $\varepsilon$-approximate first-order stationary point in $\mathrm{O}(\varepsilon^{-7/4})$ function and gradient evaluations, without using $\varepsilon$ as an input parameter. While existing methods rely on restart mechanisms to achieve this complexity, our methods do not. Consequently, the first algorithm enjoys a simple implementation, making its last iterate differentiable with respect to the initial point. By estimating the Lipschitz constants adaptively, we develop the second algorithm that does not require prior knowledge of the constants. This algorithm exhibits better numerical performance than existing parameter-free methods for certain problems, which can be attributed to its restart-free design. Both algorithms are derived by discretizing a newly introduced continuous-time model represented by an ordinary differential equation, and their continuous- and discrete-time convergence analyses proceed in a parallel manner under the Performance Estimation Problem framework.

</details>


### [349] [Relative Weak Convexity and Projected Subgradient Methods: Analysis and Convergence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30138)
*Morteza Rahimi,Masoud Ahookhosh*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一类相对弱凸函数（通过距离生成函数度量非凸性），建立了其基本性质、刻画定理、微积分法则和示例，分析了优化景观并识别出全局极小值集合附近无鞍点的邻域，提出了带多种步长策略的投影次梯度算法（PSGA），在尖锐误差界条件下证明了算法从该邻域出发收敛到全局极小值，且几何衰减步长策略可实现线性收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的弱凸性概念在度量非凸性时不够灵活，作者希望引入一个更一般化的框架——相对弱凸性，使得非凸性的度量可以依赖于一个距离生成函数，从而适用于更广泛的优化问题。

Method: 首先定义了相对弱凸函数并建立其理论性质（刻画定理、微积分法则）；然后分析其优化景观，证明全局极小值附近存在无鞍点邻域；基于此几何结构，设计了投影次梯度算法（PSGA），并提出了多种步长策略（包括几何衰减步长）。

Result: 在尖锐误差界条件下，当算法初始化于无鞍点邻域内时，PSGA在各种步长策略下均收敛到全局极小值；几何衰减步长策略可实现线性收敛速率。

Conclusion: 相对弱凸函数是一类重要的非凸函数族，其优化景观具有良好结构，PSGA算法能有效求解这类问题并保证全局收敛性。

Abstract: We introduce the class of relatively weakly convex functions, which extends the classical notion of weak convexity by measuring nonconvexity relative to a distance-generating function. We investigate the fundamental properties of this function class, establishing characterization results, calculus rules, and illustrative examples. We further analyze the associated optimization landscape and identify a neighborhood of the set of global minimizers that is free of saddle points. Motivated by this geometric structure, we propose the Projected SubGradient Algorithm (PSGA) with several step-size strategies. Under a sharpness error bound, we prove that, when initialized within this saddle-point-free neighborhood, the iterates generated by PSGA converge to a global minimizer for each of the proposed step-size strategies. Furthermore, linear convergence is established for the geometrically decaying step-size strategy.

</details>


### [350] [A survey of trust-region radius update mechanisms. Part I: First-order analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30202)
*Jérémy Rieussec,Fabian Bastin*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了信任域半径更新规则的三种结构条件（下界、收缩、受控扩展），定义了弱可容许性和强可容许性机制，证明了在不同条件下的收敛性和最优复杂度，并验证了五类机制的可容许性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为信任域方法中的半径更新规则建立统一的数学框架，识别确保收敛性和最优复杂度的核心结构条件，并统一分析现有多种半径更新机制。

Method: 提炼三种结构条件（梯度范数下界、失败迭代收缩、成功迭代受控扩展），定义弱/强可容许性；在均匀有界和线性增长模型Hessian假设下进行收敛性分析和复杂度推导；验证五类机制（固定因子、步长驱动、回溯、临界锚定、梯度缩放）的可容许性。

Result: 弱可容许性保证梯度范数趋于零；强可容许性在均匀有界模型Hessian下达到O(ε^{-2})最优一阶驻点复杂度，并将收敛性扩展到线性增长模型Hessian；证明了回溯更新在线性增长模型Hessian下的收敛性；将Curtis-Scheinberg和Wang-Yuan框架扩展到三个不同缩放因子和解耦步长接受（含η=0）。

Conclusion: 所提出的结构条件为信任域半径更新提供了统一的收敛性和复杂度分析框架，多种现有机制均满足这些条件，实现了理论分析的泛化与整合。

Abstract: We isolate three structural conditions on trust-region radius update rules for smooth unconstrained nonlinear optimisation, and study the class of mechanisms they define. The conditions act on the radius directly: a lower bound relative to the gradient norm, a contraction on unsuccessful iterations, and a controlled expansion on successful ones. A mechanism is \emph{weakly admissible} if it satisfies the first two conditions, and \emph{strongly admissible} if it satisfies the lower bound together with the controlled-expansion condition. Under uniformly bounded model Hessians, weak admissibility yields $\lim_{k\to\infty}\|\nabla f(x_k)\|=0$, and strong admissibility yields the optimal worst-case complexity $O(ε^{-2})$ for first-order stationarity. Strong admissibility extends the convergence guarantee to linearly growing model Hessians. We verify admissibility for five mechanism classes: fixed-factor, step-driven, retrospective, criticality-anchored, and gradient-scaled. Along the way, we prove convergence of the retrospective update under linearly growing model Hessians and revisit the framework of Curtis and Scheinberg (2020), and Wang and Yuan (2022): we extend it to three distinct scaling factors with decoupled step acceptance (covering $η= 0$), and specialise its stochastic version to the deterministic gradient-scaled

</details>


### [351] [Fast Adaptive Tensor Methods Under Local Smoothness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30225)
*Sadok Jerad*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出了一种新的快速自适应正则化方法，在局部Lipschitz光滑条件下，非凸问题达到最优复杂度𝒪(|log(ε)|ε^{-(p+1)/p})，凸问题达到𝒪(|log(ε)|ε^{-1/p})，匹配全局Lipschitz下的标准张量方法（仅差对数项）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自适应正则化方法通常假设全局Lipschitz光滑性，而实际中光滑性可能是局部的。本文旨在提出一种在局部Lipschitz光滑条件下仍能保持最优收敛率的快速自适应正则化方法。

Method: 遵循标准张量方法的框架，但采用适当选择的正则化项和相应修改，使得在p阶张量的局部Lipschitz光滑性假设下仍能保证最优复杂度。

Result: 非凸问题：达到𝒪(|log(ε)|ε^{-(p+1)/p})复杂度以获取一阶ε-稳定点；凸问题：𝒪(|log(ε)|ε^{-1/p})迭代使最优间隙低于ε。复杂度与全局Lipschitz下的标准张量方法匹配（仅差对数项）。数值实验展示了在非凸回归问题上的潜力。

Conclusion: 提出的自适应正则化方法在局部光滑性假设下达到了与全局光滑性下标准张量方法几乎一致的最优复杂度，拓展了自适应正则化方法的适用条件。

Abstract: A new, fast adaptive regularization methods is proposed and analyzed under local Lipschitz smoothness of the $p$-th order tensor. For nonconvex problems, it achieves the optimal $\mathcal{O}\!\left(|\log(ε)|ε^{-(p+1)/p}\right)$ complexity to obtain first-order $ε$-stationary points and in the convex case, it yields $\mathcal{O}\!\left(|\log(ε)|ε^{-1/p}\right)$ iterations to drive the optimality gap below $ε$, thus matching the complexity bounds of standard tensor methods under global Lipschitz smoothness yp to logarithmic terms. The proposed algorithm follows the line of standard tensor methods with an appropriately chosen regularization and suitable modifications. Initial numerical experiments and comparisons for some nonconvex regression problems are made with the standard adaptive cubic regularization where we showcase some potential of the proposed method.

</details>


### [352] [When Natural Variables Are Not Enough: Teaching Integer Programming with Sudoku](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30227)
*Aled Williams*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文以数独为教学案例，比较了两种整数线性规划建模方法：二元分配变量模型（虽不直观但约束简单且易于扩展）和整数单元变量模型（直观但需通过大M不等式表达全异约束，导致大量成对析取约束）。同时提供了AMPL代码、解唯一性验证IP模型，并以广义数独作为计算复杂性的入门案例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通过数独这一简洁熟悉的场景，教学性地展示整数线性规划中一个深刻教训：最自然的决策变量并不总能产生有效或便捷的线性模型。

Method: 比较两种数独的整数线性规划建模方法：(1) 二元赋值变量模型——每个单元格-数字对使用二进制变量，约束简单；(2) 自然整数变量模型——每个单元格使用一个整数变量表示值，但需通过大M不等式线性化大量成对析取约束来表达全异约束。同时给出AMPL代码实现及解唯一性验证的第二个整数规划。

Result: 二元赋值变量模型约束更简单、易于扩展到变体（如杀手数独）；自然整数变量模型需大量成对析取约束和大M不等式，模型更复杂。

Conclusion: 最自然的变量选择不一定带来最优的线性规划模型；同时以广义数独作为计算复杂性理论的入门点，并强调标准9×9数独本身并非渐近问题类。

Abstract: Sudoku is a compact and familiar setting for teaching a surprisingly deep lesson in integer linear programming, namely that the most natural decision variables are not always enough to produce an effective or convenient linear model. This paper compares two formulations of Sudoku. The first uses binary assignment variables indicating whether a particular digit is assigned to a particular cell. This formulation is less natural from the perspective of the puzzle board itself, but it encodes the puzzle rules through simple assignment constraints and extends easily to variants such as Killer Sudoku. The second formulation uses the more natural approach of assigning one integer variable to represent the value in each cell, but then the central requirement is that the values in each row, column, and block must be all different. When this all-different requirement must be expressed using linear constraints while retaining the cell-value variables, the formulation becomes a large collection of pairwise disjunctions linearised by big-M inequalities. The paper includes AMPL code for both the assignment model and the natural all-different model, and derives a second integer program for certifying uniqueness. The paper also uses generalised Sudoku as a careful entry point to computational complexity, while emphasising that the standard nine-by-nine puzzle is not itself an asymptotic problem class.

</details>


### [353] [A Distributionally Robust Framework for Learned Reconstructions in Inverse Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30230)
*Floor van Maarschalkerwaart,Subhadip Mukherjee,Christoph Brune,Marcello Carioni*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结构化分布鲁棒优化（DRO）框架，通过将模糊集限制为与数据采集过程对齐的结构化扰动（如条件分布P(Y|X)），来学习对分布偏移具有鲁棒性的重建算子。该框架建立了强对偶性，推导了显式对偶表示，并给出了诱导Tikhonov正则化的最坏情况风险界。在去模糊和正弦图-to-CT重建任务中，该方法相比标准DRO和MSE基线展现出更好的鲁棒性、稳定性和可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准的经验风险最小化方法在噪声分布发生变化时泛化能力差，而传统Wasserstein DRO对所有联合分布进行均匀扰动，忽略了测量过程的物理结构，导致过于保守。

Method: 提出结构化DRO框架，将模糊集限制在P(Y|X)等子集上；建立强对偶性并推导了联合、边缘、条件分布扰动的显式有限维对偶表示；得到诱导Tikhonov正则化的最坏情况风险界。

Result: 在去模糊和正弦图-to-CT重建任务上，相比标准DRO和MSE基线具有更好的鲁棒性、稳定性和可解释性。在线性设置下，学习到的算子退化为低秩结构，恢复出数据驱动的截断SVD正则化。

Conclusion: 结构化DRO通过引入物理感知的模糊集约束，在保持鲁棒性的同时减少了标准DRO的保守性，为逆问题中的鲁棒学习提供了理论基础和实用方法。

Abstract: Learned reconstruction operators for inverse problems are typically trained under a fixed noise model, and generalize poorly when the distribution during testing differs from the one assumed during training. Distributionally robust optimization (DRO) addresses this by optimizing against the worst-case distribution within a prescribed ambiguity set, but standard Wasserstein DRO perturbs the full joint distribution uniformly, which can be overly conservative and ignores the physics of the measurement process. We develop a structured DRO framework in which the ambiguity set is restricted to structured perturbations aligned with the data-acquisition process. This allows us to learn data-driven reconstruction operators that remain robust to distributional shifts. By constraining perturbations to subsets such as $P(Y|X)$, our framework models uncertainty in the forward operator and noise model more faithfully, accommodating any noise model expressible as a stochastic forward operator. We establish strong duality for this general formulation and derive explicit finite-dimensional dual representations for perturbations in the joint, marginal, and conditional distributions. A central result is an explicit worst-case risk bound that induces Tikhonov regularization on the Lipschitz constant of the reconstruction operator, and is less conservative relative to standard DRO for well-posed problems. Numerical experiments on deblurring and sinogram-to-CT reconstruction demonstrate improved robustness, stability, and interpretability over standard DRO and MSE baselines. In the linear setting, the learned operator becomes effectively low-rank, truncating at the intrinsic dimension of the data and recovering a data-driven analogue of truncated-SVD regularization.

</details>


### [354] [Global $o(1/k^2)$ Merit Complexity of Regularized Newton Methods for Convex Multiobjective Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30250)
*Yuqia Wu,Yue Wang,Yaohua Hu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了一种用于无约束凸多目标优化的正则化牛顿方法，证明了Tanabe型惩罚函数的全局渐近衰减率为$o(1/k^2)$，并通过一维双目标反例表明指数2在一致多项式意义下是本质最优的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有凸多目标优化方法在收敛速率方面缺乏理论保证，尤其是缺乏高阶收敛性分析。作者希望将单目标情形下正则化牛顿法的$o(1/k^2)$加速结果推广到多目标场景。

Method: 提出一种迭代方法，每步最小化局部二次模型的二次正则化max-envelope函数；利用Tanabe型惩罚函数作为评价指标进行分析；在初始分量下层集紧致和Hessian Lipschitz连续的假设下推导全局收敛率；构造一维凸双目标反例证明一致多项式收敛阶的不可改进性。

Result: Tanabe型惩罚函数以$o(1/k^2)$的全局渐近速率衰减；构造的反例表明不存在一致的$\mathcal O(k^{-(2+\delta)})$估计（对任意$\delta>0$），因此指数2在一致多项式意义下是紧的。

Conclusion: 正则化牛顿法在多目标和单目标情形下均能达到$o(1/k^2)$的渐近收敛速率，且该速率在一致多项式意义下本质上是最优的，不能再被改进。

Abstract: We investigate a regularized Newton method for unconstrained convex multi-objective optimization with twice continuously differentiable objectives whose Hessians are Lipschitz continuous. At each iteration, the method minimizes the quadratically regularized max-envelope of the local quadratic models. Using a Tanabe-type merit function, we prove that this merit decays at the global asymptotic rate $o(1/k^2)$ under the compactness assumption on the initial component-wise lower level set. This result also covers the single-objective case as a special case. Finally, we construct an explicit one-dimensional convex bi-objective family showing that no uniform merit estimate of order $\mathcal O(k^{-(2+δ)})$ can hold for any fixed $δ>0$. Thus the exponent $2$ is essentially sharp in the uniform polynomial sense, despite the $o(1/k^2)$ decay on each fixed trajectory.

</details>


### [355] [Preference-Based Reward Learning under Partial Observability with Inexact Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30271)
*Reza Zolnouri,Semih Cayci*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究在不完全可观测和潜在状态推断不精确的条件下，基于偏好的奖励学习问题。通过分析有限log-linear POMDP和神经softmax POMDP模型中信念滤波器的稳定性，推导了轨迹级特征扰动的误差界，并给出了约束Bradley-Terry奖励估计的有限样本保证，将统计误差与不可约的模型失配偏差解耦。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究部分可观测性和不精确的潜在状态推断如何影响从偏好中学习奖励，澄清在非完美动力学条件下偏好奖励学习的可行性边界。

Method: 1) 在有限log-linear POMDP中，利用混合条件建立信念滤波器对参数模型误差的稳定性，给出期望和高概率下的信念失配界；2) 将稳定性机制扩展到过参数化神经网络的神经softmax POMDP模型；3) 将信念误差传播为轨迹级特征扰动；4) 推导约束Bradley-Terry奖励估计的有限样本保证。

Result: 给出了信念失配的期望界和高概率界；将统计误差与不可约模型失配偏差解耦；阐明了部分可观测性下偏好奖励学习的可行性条件。

Conclusion: 偏好奖励学习在部分可观测且动力学不完美的情况下仍然可行，但存在一个不可约的模型失配偏差，统计误差与模型误差被成功解耦。

Abstract: In this paper, we study how partial observability and inexact latent-state inference affect reward learning from preferences. To that end, we study preference-based reward learning under partial observability, where the learner forms latent-state estimates using an inexact learned POMDP model, so model error can accumulate over time. For finite log-linear POMDPs, we characterize this error term by establishing the stability of the belief filter to parametric model error under certain mixing conditions, yielding bounds on the belief mismatch in expectation and in high probability. We further extend this stability mechanism beyond the log-linear setting to neural-softmax POMDP models with overparameterized neural networks. We then propagate these errors into trajectory-level feature perturbations and derive finite-sample guarantees for constrained Bradley--Terry reward estimation from preferences. Our results decouple statistical error from an irreducible model-mismatch bias, and clarify when preference-based reward learning remains feasible under partial observability with imperfect dynamics.

</details>


### [356] [Continuous-Time Information Design for Hurricane Evacuation: Disclosure, Congestion, and Optimal Phasing under Model Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30320)
*Furkan Sezer*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了连续时间信息设计在紧急疏散中的应用，通过公共建议精度（信息设计）和分级发布调度两个杠杆引导战略疏散区域。研究发现：分层疏散命令优于同步建议；信息精确度存在符号模糊性（信息布拉思效应）；校准至飓风丽塔数据后，最优政策消除了几乎所有在途拥堵暴露，社会成本降低89%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统紧急疏散策略中，信息发布精度与同步疏散之间存在根本性矛盾：更精确的信息减少了误报性疏散，但会同步化真实疏散行为，而凸性拥堵成本惩罚这种同步化。现有研究缺乏对连续时间信息设计框架下这一权衡的系统分析。

Method: 采用连续时间信息设计框架，将应急管理机构建模为Stackelberg领导者，疏散区域为跟随者。潜在风暴建模为带快速增强时期的跳扩散过程，区域间共享走廊上存在容量约束的拥堵博弈。使用精确有限维信念滤波器追踪风暴状态，通过Isaacs方程刻画分布鲁棒相对熵问题。

Result: (i)跟随者博弈可简化为凸控制问题；(ii)领导者问题由Isaacs方程表征，值函数为唯一粘性解；(iii)无转移支付时，最优信息设计充当二阶最优拥堵收费。校准结果表明：最优政策消除几乎所有在途拥堵，社会成本降低89%；仅分层披露降低70%；信息精确度存在符号模糊性（信息布拉思效应）。

Conclusion: 分层疏散命令内生地成为最优信息设计；模糊建议在缺乏分级调度时是最优的；信息设计可作为拥堵付费的替代机制实现二阶最优。

Abstract: We study continuous-time information design for emergency evacuation, where an Emergency Management Agency (the Stackelberg leader) steers strategic evacuation zones via two levers: public advisory precision (information design) and a tiered release schedule. The latent storm is a jump-diffusion process with publicly observed rapid-intensification epochs tracked by an exact finite-dimensional belief filter. Zones play a capacity-constrained congestion game on shared corridors with belief-weighted hazard exposure. The running cost couples beliefs to a convex congestion externality, making disclosure double-edged: sharper information reduces false-alarm departures but synchronizes genuine ones, and convex congestion penalizes that synchronization. We prove that: (i) the followers' game admits a potential reduction to a convex control problem; (ii) the leader's distributionally robust relative-entropy problem is characterized by an Isaacs equation whose value is the unique viscosity solution, with verification valid for non-smooth bang-bang feedback; and (iii) without transfers, the leader's first-order condition retains an equilibrium-response term, positioning optimal information design as a second-best congestion toll. Structurally, we show that a staggered evacuation order dominates simultaneous advisories; phased evacuation emerges endogenously as optimal information design. Furthermore, public-signal precision is sign-ambiguous due to an informational Braess effect, where vague advisories are optimal unless complemented by a staggered order. Calibrated to Hurricane Rita using NHC archives, TxDOT capacities, and HRRC surveys, the model reproduces the observed gridlock along the Interstate 45 (I-45) evacuation corridor in Texas. The optimal policy removes essentially all in-transit congestion exposure, reducing social cost by 89%, while staggered disclosure alone yields a 70% reduction.

</details>


### [357] [Value Functions of Separable Convex Integer Programs are Periodically Convex](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30330)
*Koen Ligthart*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文证明整数规划的值函数在特定子域上具有周期性凸性，并将该性质从线性目标函数推广到可分离凸目标函数，进而应用于块结构整数规划的固定参数可解算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将Eisenbrand和Rothvoss针对线性目标函数建立的值函数周期性凸性，推广到更广泛的可分离凸目标函数类。

Method: 首先证明沿直线的周期性凸性等价于膨胀多面体的整数分解性质；随后利用Graver基技术将一维周期性凸性扩展到任意固定维度的域。

Result: 1. 对任意可分离凸目标函数，值函数在形如r+Mℤ^m的子域上具有凸可延拓性；2. 两阶段随机整数规划和n-折叠整数规划在特定参数化下获得FPT算法，运行时间指数级优于先前算法，且目标函数从线性推广到可分离凸。

Conclusion: 周期性凸性为块结构整数规划提供了统一的FPT算法框架，显著扩展了可优化的目标函数类并提升了算法效率。

Abstract: We consider the periodic behavior of the value functions $b\mapsto\min\{f(x)\ \vert\ Ax=b,\,x\in\mathbb Z_{\ge0}^n\}$ of integer programs. We show that there exists a positive integer $M$ depending only on the constraint matrix $A\in\mathbb Z^{m\times n}$ so that the value function is convex extensible on any subdomain of the form $r+M\mathbb Z^m$ for any $r\in\mathbb Z^m$ and any separable convex objective function $f$. With this, we extend the known periodic convexity of such functions for linear objective functions $f$, as established by Eisenbrand and Rothvoss (SODA 25), to the broader class of separable convex objective functions. We derive our main periodic convexity result by first showing that periodic convexity along lines is equivalent to the integer decomposition property of dilated polyhedra. Subsequently, we use Graver basis techniques to extend the $1$-dimensional periodic convexity to domains of arbitrary fixed dimension. We apply this periodic convexity to show that value function reformulations of block-structured integer programs become periodically convex, which yields fixed-parameter tractable (FPT) algorithms. More specifically, we optimize two-stage stochastic integer programs and $n$-fold integer programs in FPT time when parameterized by the block dimensions and coefficient size of the local blocks of the constraint matrix, allowing the coefficients of the global blocks to be large. In the setting of this parameterization, which was recently introduced by Cslovjecsek, Koutecký, Lassota, Pilipczuk, and Polak (TheoretiCS 2025), our algorithms exponentially improve on the running times of the previous optimization algorithms and extend the class of objective functions that can be optimized from linear to separable convex.

</details>


### [358] [Local-Minima-Preserving Continuous Relaxation of Ising Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30333)
*Debraj Banerjee,Santanu Mahapatra,Kunal N. Chaudhury*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对广义Ising问题，构建了一个多项式松弛，并证明了景观等价定理：松弛问题的局部极小值与原始Ising问题的单翻转局部极小值一一对应。这一发现将Ising问题转化为求解光滑函数的局部极小值，从而可以利用基于梯度的优化器（如ADAM）进行求解。实验表明，该方法在自旋玻璃模型、MAX-CUT和NPP等挑战性基准上具有可扩展性和强性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广义Ising问题涵盖了大量困难的组合优化问题（如MAX-CUT、数划分、最大独立集），但直接求解困难。本文旨在通过构建一个光滑松弛，将原始离散优化问题转化为可用梯度方法求解的连续优化问题。

Method: 提出一个多项式松弛，并严格证明景观等价定理，建立松弛问题局部极小值与原始问题单翻转局部极小值之间的一一对应关系；进而采用梯度优化器（如ADAM）求解。

Result: 该方法在自旋玻璃模型、MAX-CUT和数划分（NPP）等基准测试中取得了强性能，且具有良好的可扩展性。

Conclusion: 通过构建光滑松弛和景观等价性，将Ising问题有效转化为梯度可优化问题，为求解大规模组合优化问题提供了新途径。

Abstract: The generalized Ising problem captures a broad spectrum of hard combinatorial problems, including MAX-CUT, Number Partitioning (NPP), and Maximum Independent Set. In this work, we consider the notion of one-flip local minima for this problem. We construct a polynomial relaxation and prove the landscape equivalence theorem: there exists a one-to-one correspondence between the local minima of the relaxation and the one-flip minima of the original Ising problem. This guarantee reduces the Ising problem to finding the local minima of a smooth function, allowing us to leverage gradient-based optimizers such as ADAM. We demonstrate that our method is scalable and it achieves strong performance across challenging benchmarks, including spin-glass models, MAX-CUT, and NPP.

</details>


### [359] [Infinite-Horizon Linear-Quadratic Difference Games with Coupled Affine Inequality Constraints: Open-Loop Generalized Nash Equilibria](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30343)
*Partha Sarathi Mohapatra*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了具有耦合仿射不等式约束的确定性无限时域线性二次差分博弈，推导了开环广义纳什均衡存在的必要条件，并在附加假设下证明了其充分性，通过将问题转化为大规模线性互补问题来求解，最后用车辆队列示例验证了结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究对带有耦合状态-控制约束的无限时域线性二次差分博弈的开环广义纳什均衡缺乏系统的理论分析，特别是在必要条件和充分条件的建立以及计算方法的可行性方面存在空白。

Method: 通过关联两个无限时域耦合线性互补系统的平方可和解，推导开环广义纳什均衡的必要条件和充分条件；将均衡求解问题重新表述为大规模线性互补问题。

Result: 获得了开环广义纳什均衡存在的必要条件和附加假设下的充分条件；证明了计算问题可转化为大规模线性互补问题求解；车辆队列示例验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文为带有耦合约束的无限时域线性二次差分博弈提供了理论分析与计算框架，为实际应用（如车辆队列控制）提供了数学基础。

Abstract: In this technical note, we study a class of deterministic infinite-horizon linear-quadratic difference games with coupled affine inequality constraints involving both state and control variables. We derive necessary conditions for the existence of open-loop generalized Nash equilibria and establish their sufficiency under additional assumptions by relating square-summable solutions of two associated infinite-horizon coupled linear complementarity systems. We further reformulate these conditions and show that computing open-loop generalized Nash equilibria reduces to solving a large-scale linear complementarity problem together with verifying additional conditions. Finally, we illustrate our results using a vehicle platooning example with constraints.

</details>


### [360] [A note on the maximal covering location problem with customer preference ordering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30379)
*Elisabeth Gaar,Markus Sinnl*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文证明带顾客偏好排序的最大覆盖选址问题（MCLP-PO）可被轻松转化为经典最大覆盖选址问题（MCLP），且转化后实例规模减小，从而可直接使用经典MCLP的现有算法求解。计算实验表明，使用精确算法时该转化能带来至少一个数量级的加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期一系列论文针对带顾客偏好排序的最大覆盖选址问题提出了混合整数双层优化模型、单层重构以及多种启发式算法（GRASP、Tabu搜索、变邻域搜索），但作者发现该问题实际上可简化为经典MCLP，无需设计复杂的专用算法。

Method: 通过数学推导证明MCLP-PO实例到经典MCLP实例的变换关系，并说明变换后实例规模（设施数量、顾客数量、覆盖矩阵）会减小。随后通过计算实验对比直接求解与变换后求解的性能差异。

Result: 变换后的经典MCLP实例规模更小，使用精确算法求解时速度提升至少一个数量级。

Conclusion: 带顾客偏好排序的最大覆盖选址问题本质上并未引入新的计算复杂性，可直接利用经典MCLP的算法高效求解，先前文献中开发的专用算法是不必要的。

Abstract: Recently a series of papers introduced and investigated the maximal covering location problem with customer preference ordering, a variant of the classical maximal covering location problem (MCLP). In these papers, mixed-integer bilevel optimization models and single-level reformulations were presented for this problem, as well as various heuristics such as a GRASP, a Tabu search and a variable neighborhood search. In this short note we show that instances of this new problem can actually be easily transformed into instances of the classical MCLP and this transformation even reduces the size of the instance. Thus, existing algorithms for the classical MCLP can be used to solve it. We provide a short computational study to show that this transformation leads to speed-ups of at least a magnitude when considering exact algorithms.

</details>


### [361] [Discovering the Kalman-Bucy-Koopman Filter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30487)
*Umesh Vaidya*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于Koopman算子谱理论的新型非线性状态估计框架——Kalman-Bucy-Koopman (KBK)滤波器，通过将非线性估计问题转化为Koopman坐标下的Riccati型演化，实现了对经典Kalman-Bucy滤波器的线性算子类比，同时保留原始状态变量的非线性结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统非线性状态估计方法（如扩展卡尔曼滤波）依赖模型线性化，在强非线性系统中性能退化严重。本文旨在利用Koopman算子理论，将非线性估计问题转化为线性框架下的可解形式，同时保持原始非线性动力学结构。

Method: 将非线性估计问题表述为最大似然（Mortensen）估计器，其解由Hamilton-Jacobi偏微分方程刻画；利用主Koopman本征函数参数化HJ值函数，将非线性估计问题转化为Koopman坐标下的Riccati型演化；提出路径积分公式计算主Koopman本征函数，并引入基于特征构造的动力学信息基函数用于近似。

Result: 推导了值函数和状态估计近似的理论误差界；仿真结果表明该方法在性能上优于扩展卡尔曼滤波器，且能够在无显式模型线性化的数据驱动场景下运行。

Conclusion: KBK滤波器为非线性状态估计提供了一种新的算子谱理论框架，兼具线性滤波器的计算结构与非线性动力学的表达能力，适用于数据驱动的非线性估计任务。

Abstract: This paper introduces the Kalman-Bucy-Koopman (KBK) filter, a novel framework for nonlinear state estimation grounded in Koopman operator spectral theory. The nonlinear estimation problem is formulated as a maximum-likelihood (Mortensen) estimator whose solution is characterized by a Hamilton-Jacobi (HJ) partial differential equation. The proposed KBK filter provides a spectral, operator-theoretic realization of this nonlinear filtering problem by parameterizing the HJ value function in terms of principal Koopman eigenfunctions. This transformation converts the nonlinear estimation problem into a Riccati-type evolution in Koopman coordinates, yielding a linear-operator analogue of the classical Kalman-Bucy filter while preserving nonlinear structure in the original state variables. We develop a path-integral formulation for computing principal Koopman eigenfunctions and introduce a dynamics-informed, characteristics-inspired basis construction for their approximation. Theoretical error bounds are derived for value-function and state-estimation approximations. Simulation results demonstrate improved performance over the extended Kalman filter and illustrate the ability of the KBK framework to operate in data-driven settings without explicit model linearization.

</details>


### [362] [Robust McKean--Vlasov Variational Systems with Asymmetric Loss Aversion: Well-Posedness, Stability, and Propagation of Chaos for the Forward and Regularized Backward Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30526)
*Tcheick Kayembe,Rostin Mabela,Jean-Pierre Bosonga,Eugène Mbuyi*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了一类在非主导概率测度族模型不确定性下的鲁棒前向-后向McKean-Vlasov变分系统，通过Yosida正则化、Minty-Brézis识别等技巧建立了解的存在唯一性与稳定性，并针对前向动力学证明了粒子逼近和传播混沌的显式收敛率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在模型不确定性（非主导概率测度族）下，研究具有非光滑凸泛函（刻画非对称损失厌恶）的鲁棒前向-后向McKean-Vlasov变分系统，填补该领域在鲁棒性、非光滑性和均场相互作用共存时的理论空白。

Method: 采用Wasserstein空间中的不动点论证建立前向动力学的存在唯一性与稳定性；将后向分量构造为选定的后向变分系统（非经典BSVI）；使用Yosida正则化、一致先验估计、正则化解收敛性以及Minty-Brézis识别论证得到与最小范数选择关联的典范解；构建粒子逼近并证明传播混沌及显式收敛率。

Result: 建立了鲁棒前向动力学的存在唯一性与稳定性；给出了后向变分系统的典范解；证明了前向动力学在非主导测度族上一致收敛的传播混沌；对固定正则化参数的后向分量建立了定量传播混沌，并指出在非光滑阈值附近需要额外非接触假设才能获得一致估计。

Conclusion: 本文为具有非对称损失厌恶和非主导模型不确定性的均场变分系统提供了完整的理论框架，但正则化参数与粒子数一致均匀的估计需要额外的非接触条件。

Abstract: We study a class of robust forward--backward McKean--Vlasov variational systems under model uncertainty represented by a non-dominated family of probability measures. Mean-field interactions are described through nonlinear collective observables acting on the laws of the forward and backward components. To model asymmetric loss aversion, we introduce a nonsmooth convex functional whose subdifferential defines a law-dependent maximal monotone operator acting on the forward state. We establish existence, uniqueness, and stability of the robust forward dynamics by a fixed-point argument in Wasserstein space. The backward component is formulated as a selected backward variational system rather than a classical backward stochastic variational inequality. Our analysis relies on Yosida regularization, uniform a priori estimates, convergence of the regularized solutions, and a Minty--Brézis identification argument, yielding a canonical solution associated with the minimal norm selection. We further construct a particle approximation and prove propagation of chaos for the forward dynamics with explicit convergence rates uniformly over the non-dominated family. For each fixed regularization parameter, we also establish quantitative propagation of chaos for the regularized backward component and explain why estimates uniform in both the number of particles and the regularization parameter require additional non-contact assumptions near the nonsmooth threshold.

</details>
